[dcheck-to-check] Convert DCHECKs in chrome/browser/ [25/25]

As part of effort to harden Chromium's safety, turn DCHECKs
into CHECKs. The converted CHECKs are not fatal until M161.

See also:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1tzGQwGG0lJ6K7Ur8wBW6E58CvJc1JQNyiX0ryHe7A5I/edit?usp=sharing

This patch is generated by the following script:
https://crrev.com/c/7849874/18/tools/memory/dcheck/replace_dchecks_with_checks.py

PSA:
https://groups.google.com/a/chromium.org/g/chromium-dev/c/vd1Qt-egELY/m/badCjDnsDAAJ
LSC (Google internal):
http://goto.google.com/chrome-lsc-dcheck-to-check-conversions

Binary-Size: Increase is temporary until NotFatalUntil removal.

[Gardener] Disable failing tast.shimlessrma.CancelFlow.select_components_none_next_cancel_critical

The test tast.shimlessrma.CancelFlow.select_components_none_next_cancel_critical
has been failing consistently across builds on chromeos-volteer-chrome-preuprev
(#3781, #3782) due to ChromeOS system daemon rmad failing during D-Bus
startup (pthread_create EAGAIN), causing the Shimless RMA WebUI landing page
to hang and timing out the 30s UI heading poll.

Disabling the test in chrome_all_tast_tests to restore CI tree health while
the underlying platform daemon issue is investigated.

R=dljames@chromium.org, fergal@chromium.org, yoshiki@chromium.org

tree
c2cc7c67f3034531bad188f65c76d294dda53c67
parent
da47418dae9338ba98060550b51ba64e1ec2b249 [ diff ] 
Replicate web test runtime flags to all live frames across windows

In WebTestControlHost::WebTestRuntimeFlagsChanged(), runtime flag
changes were previously sent to only the first frame found per process
in `web_test_render_frame_map_`. With RenderDocument, a same-process
navigation creates a new RenderFrameHost while the previous
RenderFrameHost is still in the map (and still considered live by the
browser) even after its renderer-side WebFrameTestProxy has been swapped
out. Messages sent to the old frame's remote are silently dropped, and
the new frame isn't in `web_test_render_frame_map_` yet, so the target
renderer process never receives the updated flags (such as
`dump_as_text` or `dump_child_frames`).

https://crrev.com/c/5368628 previously fixed this by iterating over all
live frames (including speculative ones) of `main_window_`, but was
reverted in https://crrev.com/c/5366211 because DevTools web tests run
the test script in `main_window_` (the DevTools frontend) and the
inspected page in `secondary_window_`, so only iterating `main_window_`
dropped flag updates (such as `setDumpConsoleMessages(false)`) meant for
`secondary_window_` (crbug.com/329518758).

Walk all live frames (including speculative ones) across all `Shell`
windows instead, and remove the flaky expectation for
xss-DENIED-to-data-url-in-foreign-domain-subframe-location-change.html.

[dcheck-to-check] Convert DCHECKs in chrome/browser/ [20/25]

As part of effort to harden Chromium's safety, turn DCHECKs
into CHECKs. The converted CHECKs are not fatal until M161.

See also:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1tzGQwGG0lJ6K7Ur8wBW6E58CvJc1JQNyiX0ryHe7A5I/edit?usp=sharing

eef334adabf1e96b203a3b2df52807ee758d6334 [ diff ] 
Rename ADVANCED_MEMORY_SAFETY_CHECKS to SANITIZED_OBJECT

This is a mechanical rewrite change.

Rename the `ADVANCED_MEMORY_SAFETY_CHECKS` macro to `SANITIZED_OBJECT`,
`INHERIT_MEMORY_SAFETY_CHECKS` to `INHERIT_SANITIZED_OBJECT`, and
`DEFAULT_MEMORY_SAFETY_CHECKS` to `NO_SANITIZED_OBJECT`.

Also rename `base/memory/advanced_memory_safety_checks.{h,cc}` to
`base/memory/sanitized_object.{h,cc}` and update remaining
`AdvancedMemorySafetyChecks` / `advanced_memory_safety_checks`
identifiers in `base/`, `partition_alloc/`, and `PRESUBMIT.py`.

Here is the script AI used to rewrite the annotation users:
```
import subprocess
files = [ ... ] # 54 caller files outside base/
for f in files:
    with open(f, "r") as fp:
        content = fp.read()
    new_content = content.replace(
        "#include \"base/memory/advanced_memory_safety_checks.h\"",
        "#include \"base/memory/sanitized_object.h\""
    ).replace("ADVANCED_MEMORY_SAFETY_CHECKS", "SANITIZED_OBJECT")
    with open(f, "w") as fp:
        fp.write(new_content)
subprocess.run(["git", "cl", "format"], check=True)
```

TAG=agy
CONV=1688e889-a808-401a-854c-f80e12b2fe32

tree
802aad8cd24903dc88adf537000cbc8b27d832d3
parent
8758e8b9a6e53fbd071c183db4ecc0db1a599611 [ diff ] 
Drop component and export settings from resource_broker mojom

The generated headers include content/common/content_export.h, but
mojom() targets cannot depend on //content:export, so they fail
gn check --check-generated.

The only user, //content/services/resource_broker, is linked into
//content, so the bindings do not need to be an exported component.
Build them as a plain source_set.

Continues the incremental `chrome.cookies` migration groundwork
by removing its remaining dependency on `Profile`.

instead of Profile.

delegate to remove the remaining dependencies on Profile.

PrefetchBurstQp: Add burst support for prefetches with query param

This CL introduces `kPrefetchSchedulerBurstQueryParam` feature and its
params (`key`, `value`, `extra_limit`). When enabled, prefetches
matching the query parameter are assigned burst priority
`kBurstForQueryParam` and the active set limit for burst is extended by
`extra_limit`.

tree
7daaceef4b3cb0de932fbeee52eb590018ee4289
parent
3ab180b95b2502e342b036192d09d034e8fd4124 [ diff ] 
FileSystemAccess: Fix flakiness in FilePathWatcher tests

The previous CL (crrev.com/c/7682142) added SpinEventLoopForABit() to
allow in-flight events to arrive. However, because tests use
AccumulatingEventExpecter which accumulates all events, trailing events
(like an IN_IGNORED after IN_DELETE) were captured during the spin.
When the test later asserted on the expected events using an exact matcher
(testing::ContainerEq), the presence of these trailing events caused the
match to fail.

This CL replaces SpinEventLoopForABit() with a new pattern:
delegate.SpinAndDiscardAllReceivedEvents()
event_expecter = AccumulatingEventExpecter();

This ensures that the event loop is pumped to establish watches, but any
trailing events are discarded before the next assertion.

Bug: 491835917

bbbdbc51667bd2e3aa2766af1c1340bac72c4781 [ diff ] 
Use DCHECK for IsSQLValid check in BtmDatabase::ClearTimestamps

In BtmDatabase::ClearTimestamps, replacing the DCHECK with a CHECK for
kClearWaaSql validity could cause crashes in release builds if the SQL
query is invalid (for example, due to database schema variations or
corruption). Reverting back to DCHECK prevents production crashes while
retaining debug assertions.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/9d239fb72f800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Mon Oct 05 02:56:17 2026
tree
564fabfaa2a51a4203b1d4b4debc669091b52cfb
parent
8d9f7ca4b77b2dae21f30f3be79531a19013572e [ diff ] 
Forbid V8 wrapper creation for PseudoElement

Because `blink::PseudoElement` inherits from `blink::Element`, it's
possible to use `ToV8Traits<Node>` or `ToV8Traits<Element>` to wrap it
in a Node or Element wrapper for JS. However, this is dangerous as
this allows JS to mutate the DOM in ways that would break invariants.

Since nothing relies on this today, and the inheritance is an internal
implementation detail rather than something meant to be web-exposed,
disallow creation of Node or Element wrappers by overriding `Wrap()` and
`AssociateWithWrapper()` and simply crash instead, similar to how
`WorkerOrWorkletGlobalScope` blocks V8 wrapper creation. Also update
`WebNode::ToV8Value()` to return an empty handle when the `WebNode`
wraps a `PseudoElement`.

mojo: Deduplicate transitive modules and kinds in mojom module pickles

When mojom_parser loads multiple .mojom-module dependencies via
Module.Load(), shared transitive imports (such as unguessable_token.mojom
or time.mojom) are unpickled as distinct Module and Kind instances from
each dependency file. When the dependent module is subsequently dumped
with pickle.dump(), these duplicate instances are all serialized
separately, causing exponential duplication across the import DAG.

For example, agent_scheduling_group.mojom-module only depends on 405
unique .mojom files, but previously contained 15,001 serialized Module
instances (including 1,495 copies of unguessable_token.mojom), bloating
the single pickle file to 101 MB.

This change introduces _MojomPickler using pickle.Pickler.reducer_override
to canonicalize duplicate Module, Kind, and NamedValue instances by their
module path and spec during Module.Dump(), while keeping .mojom-module
files completely self-contained and readable via standard pickle.load().

Results for content/common:
- agent_scheduling_group.mojom-module size: 100.9 MB -> 5.8 MB (-94.3%,
  15,001 -> 405 Module instances)
- agent_scheduling_group.mojom-module Dump time: 3.26s -> 0.13s (25.6x
  faster)
- agent_scheduling_group.mojom-module Load time: 2.51s -> 0.11s (23.0x
  faster)
- Total size across all 15 .mojom-module files in content/common:
  318.6 MB -> 20.3 MB (-93.6%)
- Total Dump time across all 15 files (single-core): 9.66s -> 0.40s
  (23.9x faster)
- content/common mojom_bindings_generator execution time:
  18.1s -> 3.7s (min 3.5s, 4.9x faster)
  Max RSS memory usage: 488 MB -> 84 MB (-82.8%)

06d34dc52ad19c986b78cd2aa96edc72d6316567 [ diff ] 
[FSA] Run after-write checks after popup teardown

When a FileSystemWritableFileStream closes or a file handle moves,
FileSystemAccessSafeMoveHelper computes the swap file hash on the IO
thread before running Safe Browsing after-write checks on the UI thread.
If the initiating popup or frame closes during write or hash
computation, its RenderFrameHost, WebContents, and RenderProcessHost are
destroyed while the move helper remains alive. Previously,
DoSafeBrowsingCheckOnUIThread looked up
RenderProcessHost::FromID(frame_id.child_id) to obtain BrowserContext.
When RenderProcessHost was null, it returned
DownloadCheckResult::UNKNOWN, which InterpretSafeBrowsingResult mapped
to kAllow (fail-open), committing the file without a Safe Browsing
check.

To fix this, snapshot WebContents (as a WeakPtr) and
outermost_main_frame_id when FileSystemAccessHandleBase or
FileSystemAccessSafeMoveHelper is constructed on the UI thread, and pass
them through to FileSystemAccessWriteItem. Additionally, populate
item->browser_context from
ChromeFileSystemAccessPermissionContext::profile() in
DoSafeBrowsingCheckOnUIThread via a WeakPtr to the permission context,
and fail closed (DownloadCheckResult::BLOCKED_SCAN_FAILED -> kBlock) if
browser_context is unavailable when the UI thread check runs.

Bug: b:503201581
Test: content_unittests --gtest_filter=*FileSystemAccessSafeMoveHelper*
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=ChromeFileSystemAccessPermission*

VM_STATUS_FAILURE, or VM_STATUS_UNKNOWN, non-fatal CHECK_EQ causes
release builds to trigger a crash dump. Revert CHECK_EQ back to

crash/d9e66c5170400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

d99289ae33cb6f5f75af545e153fba4e18ff7573
parent
088a6ad1567d15409ca6b9c2cbcd3110d11f89fa [ diff ] 
[CALI] Add "Zoom Fit" to dynamically scale Screenshot to fit pane

This CL adds the "Fit" entry to the Zoom dropdown. Selecting this causes
the Screenshot to dynamically scale to fit the available viewport along
the layout orientation, while preserving the aspect ratio.

Re. terminology: "Zoom" is the UI feature / intent, and "Scale" is the
sizing multiplier applied to the Screenshot. Under Fixed Zoom, these are
the same. Under Zoom Fit, "Zoom" is the abstract idea of "Fit", and
"Scale" varies on Screenshot Pane's viewport dimensions.

WLOG, let's assume Horizontal Layout, where Screenshot Pane appears on
left / right, with a vertical Splitter that slides horizontally.

Key Features:
* Zoom "Fit" Option: Screenshot Pane width (SPW) change triggers Scale
  change: Scaled Screenshot becomes maximally wide without triggering
  horizontal scrollbar.
* Auto-Resize for Zoom "Fit": SPW is changed to produce 100% scale,
  best-effort (i.e., subject to min / max pane constraints).
* Dynamic Option label: The "Fit" dropdown item shows current Scale as
  percentage, e.g., "Fit (42.15%)". Ths requires #

Under Fixed Zoom, Auto-Resize triggers Scale (fixed) => SPW update. The
new Zoom Fit feature imposes SPW => Scale. Auto-Resize thus triggers
Scale (100%) => SPW => Scale. Getting these right requires addressing
various UI details:
* Auto-Resize Overhead: Given Scale, we compute the Scaled Screenshot
  width and change SPW to fit. However, if Scaled Screenshot *height* is
  too large, the vertical scrollbar will show -- this requires adding
  "overhead" to SPW. For Vertical Layout, we'd also add Screenshot Info
  height. `LayoutController._autoResize()` already handles this.
* Zoom Fit Scrollbar Peekaboo: Horizontal scrollbar must not be shown.
  For SPW => Scale, the "naive" Scale can similarly cause vertical
  scrollbar to show, and transitively cause horizontal scrollbar to
  show -- but we don't want that. So if this happens, we subtract
  scrollbar thickness from SPW and recompute Scale. But at boundary
  case, this can in turn make vertical scrollbar unnecessary! Thus the
  scaled Screenshot will now have awkward space around it. We solve this
  by forcing the vertical scrollbar to appear. This is handled in
  `ScreenshotVis._applyRescale()`.
* SPW Imprecision: SPW is controlled in CSS via `#div-pane-screenshot`'s
  `flex-basis` as percentage, and Chrome seems to truncate to 4 decimal
  places. Additional truncation might also happen in the browser. As a
  result, the raw SPW read might be off-integer. Now, for Auto-Resize on
  Zoom Fit we have Scale (100%) => SPW => Scale. Suppose
  Scale (100%) => SPW step yields 800px as the desired SPW. But once
  applied as `flex-basis` and read back, we get 799.984375px. When we
  resize Screenshot (canvas) we'd like integer along the layout
  direction. If we use `floor()` we'd get 799px, and now Scale becomes
  799px / 800px != 100%! Therefore we need `round()` to restore
  fidelity. But now we'd have 800px Screenshot inside 799.984375px
  Screenshot Pane; would horizontal scrollbar appear? Fortunately, it
  appears pre-rounding is also used by the browser on deciding whether
  to show the scrollbar. So this is okay. This is handled in
  `VisOptions.updateGeometry()`.

The code also handles Vertical Layout, of course. Details:
* style.css:
  * `#sel-zoom`: Increase width from `90px` to `110px`.
  * `#div-screenshot`: Add `[data-force-scroll="{x,y}"]` to force-show
    horizontal / vertical scrollbar.
* model.js:
  * `ZOOM_LEVELS`: Add `{title: 'Fit', scale: null}` at index 0.
  * Replace `ZOOM_LEVEL_DEFAULT_INDEX` with `ZOOM_FIT_INDEX = 0`.
  * `VisOptions`:
    * CTOR: Default to Zoom Fit via `zoomIndex = ZOOM_FIT_INDEX`.
    * Add `isZoomFit()` to check whether Zoom Fit mode is used.
    * Add `getAutoResizeDims()` to return the desired Scaled dimensions
      for Auto-Resize, using scale for Fixed Zoom and 100% for Zoom Fit.
    * Update `updateGeometry()`: For Zoom Fit, calculate `scale` to
      resize Screenshot to fit Screenshot Viewport [width, height] for
      [Horizontal, Vertical] Layout.
  * `ViewNode`: Remove unused property `expanded`.
* ui_common.js: Update `HINT_STRINGS[LAYOUT_SPLITTER` to include hint on
  double-click to Auto-Resize.
* layout_ui.js: `LayoutController`:
  * Update `_autoResize()`: Use `getAutoResizeDims()`.
* screenshot_ui.js:
  * `ScreenshotInfoVis`: CTOR: Add JSDoc.
  * `ScreenshotVis`:
    * CTOR: Add JSDoc.
    * Rename `_refreshScreenshotDimensions()` --> `_applyRescale()`, and
      update to handle Zoom Fit:
      * Use two-pass dimension calculation to detect and compensate for
        induced secondary scrollbar appearance, possibly force scrollbar
        appearance via `dataset.forceScroll` to handle boundary issues.
  * `ScreenshotController`:
    * CTOR: Add passed `onResize` callback.
    * Update `_bindViewportResize()`: For the resize observer lambda:
      When calling `updateScaleAndRefresh()` pass `isZoomFit()` as
      `needToRescale` to trigger dynamic resize; call `onResize()`
      always.
* app.js:
  * MainVis:
    * Update `populateZoomOptions()` to initialize selection to
      `ZOOM_FIT_INDEX`.
    * Add `updateZoomFitLabel()`: Format the "Fit" dropdown option text
      to `Fit ({#.##}%)`, when active and loaded (else reset to `Fit`).
  * MainController:
    * Wire `onResize` callback into `ScreenshotController` to trigger
      `updateZoomFitLabel()`.
    * Update `handleLoad()` and `selZoom` change event listeners to
      refresh the "Fit" label.

Deflake transition-shape-outside-crash.html

The crashtest waits for a `transitionend` event on an `<input>` element
with `autofocus` and `shape-outside: padding-box`, relying on `autofocus`
matching `:focus-visible` and changing the UA `outline` style to trigger
a CSS transition. If focus or `:focus-visible` does not produce a style
change (e.g., when the frame is not focused), no transition starts and
the test times out.

Explicitly flush the initial style and change `color` via a class so a
CSS transition on the element is guaranteed to run and fire
`transitionend` regardless of focus state, while still exercising the
`BaseComputedStyle` comparison for `shape-outside: padding-box`.

Bug: 485677942

parent
15fb8216a4057f5005e4f5f832b781dd0fdf3d0e [ diff ] 
[wpt] Deflake bidi/browsing_context/user_prompt_opened/beforeunload.py

The test cancelled the pending `browsingContext.navigate` task after
receiving `userPromptOpened`. Since beforeunload prompts are accepted by
default, the navigation can still succeed before the tab is closed in
the `new_tab` fixture teardown. In that case the WPT BiDi client calls
`set_result()` on the cancelled future, which raises InvalidStateError
and terminates the websocket reader task, so the `browsingContext.close`
response is never processed and the test hangs until the harness
timeout.

Await the navigation instead so that no command is pending at teardown.
Locally this went from ~4/25 timeouts to 0/40.

Bug: 565888355, 568343392
Change-Id: If705703539cb8b1be6c7ee4faa1ae63d3486189d

Introduce AshTestBase::SetUpInitParams(AshTestHelper::InitParams&)

Consolidate all AshTestBase::set_*** methods that mutate init_params_
(set_start_session, set_create_signin_pref_service,
set_create_global_cras_audio_handler, set_create_quick_pair_mediator,
set_add_default_shelf_icon, and set_shell_delegate) into a virtual
AshTestBase::SetUpInitParams(AshTestHelper::InitParams&) method and
remove the init_params_ member from AshTestBase.

Also remove the AshTestBase(task_environment, local_state) constructor
and instead pass TestingBrowserProcess::GetGlobal()->local_state() via
ChromeAshTestBase::SetUpInitParams.

TAG=agy
CONV=04fa7772-ef71-470e-ad43-d9d117f5c2c5

tree
76171f949e8717807fbccdd8cbf492de0d22a149
parent
e89a996c003be90b434f075a4b55b313b43ac15c [ diff ] 
Consolidate per-view maps in TextInputManager into ViewMap<ViewState>

TextInputManager previously maintained five separate ViewMap
(std::unordered_map<RenderWidgetHostViewBase*, T>) instances with
identical keys initialized in Register() and erased in Unregister():
- text_input_state_map_
- selection_region_map_
- edit_context_selection_region_map_
- composition_range_info_map_
- text_selection_map_

Consolidate these five maps into a single ViewMap<ViewState> and move
SelectionRegion::operator== out-of-line. This eliminates four duplicate
std::__hash_table template instantiations, avoids repeated per-method
hash lookups, and reduces text_input_manager.o __TEXT size by ~10.9 KB.

Bug: 568057871

d32162d544072b4fb52487db71898424b1f40596
parent
cb6eb0e7cac01aed6a7c24fc665428af94a0b578 [ diff ] 
[wpt] Unregister SW in controlled-iframe-postMessage.https.html

The test registered postMessage-client-worker.js with the broad scope
'resources' and never unregistered it. The leaked worker then
intercepted navigations under resources/ made by later tests in the
same browser session; in fetch-response-taint.https.html the login
iframe never loaded, causing a deterministic 60s TIMEOUT on first run
(and PASS on retry with a fresh profile) on Mac13 and Win11 bots.

Use service_worker_unregister_and_register(), narrow the scope to the
frame the test actually uses, and unregister when done.

43bcddeae5d0113c5214c6042bb08935c0cbf2e9
parent
ec4754d67840d555831eaf14befd6536af4e1f30 [ diff ] 
[wpt] Unregister SW in update-module-request-mode.https.html

waiting.https.html has been flaky (deterministic FAIL then PASS on
retry) on bots where it runs after update-module-request-mode.https.html
in the same browser session. The failure is:

FAIL waiting is set after installation - assert_equals: controller
  expected null but got object "[object ServiceWorker]"

update-module-request-mode.https.html registers a worker with the broad
scope 'resources/' and never unregisters it. waiting.https.html then
loads an iframe at resources/blank.html, which gets controlled by that
leaked worker; its own service_worker_unregister() call only unregisters
registrations whose scope exactly matches 'resources/blank.html', so the
leak isn't cleaned up.

1761a116292d922519542a6e9dc4873b70cd7c99

5a1c6c2fc7307d48b99aff4c361636f356a96291 [ diff ] 
[TabStripUnification] Fix touch and pen interaction on ChromeOS

On ChromeOS, real finger and pen taps routinely exceed the 6px touch
slop threshold (max_touch_move_in_pixels_for_click), which cancels
kGestureTap and dispatches kGestureScrollBegin instead.

Previously: 1. TabView::OnGestureEvent only selected tabs on kGestureTap
and ignored
   kGestureScrollBegin, allowing ScrollView to consume touch gestures even when
   not overflowing or when moving perpendicular to the scroll axis.
2. TabView::GetHitTestMask used PathType::kHighlight with a hardcoded
1.0f
   scale instead of PathType::kHitTest with the compositor device scale factor.
3. GetGestureDetail only classified kGestureTapDown as touch rather than
any
   gesture event.
4. TabGroupHeaderView passive child views intercepted hit-testing,
preventing
   touch/pen events from reaching the header view.
5. TabDragHandlerImpl::OnGestureEvent ignored kGestureScrollBegin and
did not
   forward short tap releases when dragging was initialized before drag
   threshold was reached.

This CL fixes these issues and adds interactive UI tests verifying both
finger touch (with movement exceeding touch slop) and stylus/pen touch
on horizontal and vertical tab strips.

TAG=agy
CONV=d33c5cf2-c83f-454e-a44d-361cd44b91a6
